🌟 Meet Patricia (Patty) Trapnell, our newest team member! 🌟

With 37 years of nursing experience, Patty is a Board Certified Registered Nurse specializing in medical aesthetics for 19 years. She's a Certified Aesthetic Nurse Specialist (CANS), a distinction held by fewer than 200 nurses nationwide. Patty stays ahead of the curve by attending conferences and classes, ensuring she's up-to-date with the latest advancements in aesthetics.

Prior to joining us, Patty worked in cardiology, oncology, and dermatology, bringing a wealth of diverse experience to our team. When she's not in the office, Patty enjoys exploring Oregon's outdoors with her three children and dog Lucy, indulging in running, kayaking, and hiking.

Welcome Patty, your expertise and passion are invaluable additions to our team! 🌟

Chances are, you've seen our October special for the Dracula Facial, but what IS it? 😱🦇

When you think of the name Dracula, you may think of a vampire ready to suck the life out of humans. However, the procedure itself is more formally known as Platelet-Rich Plasma (PRP) therapy, and it breathes new life into your skin. The nickname “Dracula Facial” comes from the process of using one’s own blood to enhance facial skin.

Find out more in our newest blog, "Oh my Gourd! What's a Dracula Facial?!"
👉 Click the link in our bio
👉 Head to our "Blog" tab

Meet Karla Pivik!

Dr. Karla Snider Pivik, FAAD, FAOCD, is a patient-centered Board-Certified Dermatologist hailing from the scenic town of Green River, Wyoming. With two Bachelor’s Degrees from the University of Wyoming, she pursued Osteopathic Medical School in Southern California, graduating with honors. 👏🎓

Dr. Pivik completed her residency in Oregon, embracing the osteopathic philosophy for holistic patient care, addressing both skin health and overall well-being.

Outside the office, Dr. Pivik enjoys exploring Oregon's landscapes with her husband, three active boys, and her pets. She is an outdoor enthusiast, a disc golf player, a pottery enthusiast, and a self-proclaimed campfire cuisine lover.🌲🏕️

We're incredibly proud and grateful to have Dr. Pivik as a valuable member of our team, bringing her expertise to enhance our practice at Juniper Dermatology!

You are a rockstar, Karla! 🤩👏💚

September is National Skin Care Awareness Month! Let's explore some ways you can make this National Skin Care Awareness Month your best one yet! 💁‍♀️

🌟 Adjust Your Routine for Seasonal Changes
The weather is changing! If you've been using lighter cleansers during the summer, it might be time for some thicker, more hydrating options to prepare your skin for the winter months.

🌟 Prioritize Getting A Good Night's Sleep
Getting enough sleep on a regular basis is critical in restoring your skin, preventing fine lines and wrinkles, and more. Implementing a good night-time routine can help you wind down properly for quality sleep.

🌟 Remember to Apply Sunscreen
Even if the temperatures are cooler, sunscreen is still crucial. The sun can still cause damage to our skin, even when we're indoors near windows.

🌟 Schedule a Cosmetic Dermatology Appointment
Celebrate Skin Care Awareness Month by booking a session with us! Our treatments can help rejuvenate your skin and prepare it for the colder months. Treat yourself!

Finding the right acne treatment for your skin type doesn't have to be a job only a superhero can do! 💪 The following ingredients give pesky acne a powerful punch:

💥 Benzoyl peroxide – This ingredient removes excess oil and dead skin cells while eradicating acne-causing bacteria (accessible in strengths of 2.5% to 10% without a prescription).
💥 Adapalene – A retinoid that aids in clearing pores and preventing new breakouts (available in a 0.1% strength without a prescription).
💥 Salicylic acid – Another useful compound for unclogging pores and thwarting fresh breakouts (accessible in strengths from 0.5% to 2% without a prescription).
💥 Azelaic acid – A mildly antibacterial component that prevents pore congestion and addresses post-acne dark spots (available in a 10% strength without a prescription).

Uhhh…What are Lipomas?

A lipoma is a common type of benign tumor composed of adipose (fat) tissue. These tumors are usually located just beneath the skin and look like kind of like a speed bump under your skin.

They often feel soft and rubbery to the touch and can develop anywhere on the body, though they commonly appear on the neck, shoulders, back, arms, and thighs. They may look intimidating, but these growths are typically slow-growing and rarely cause pain or discomfort.

Learn more about these "fatty bumps" in our most recent blog! 👉 Link in bio

August and September are widely known as our big wildfire season. Not only does it affect our environment, the safety of homes nearby, and the air we're breathing in, but it also affects our skin! Yep, you heard us right. 😳

It has to do with oxidative stress. Wildfire smoke and pollution can damage your DNA, break down collagen, and compromise your skin barrier.

So, what can you do about it?

🔹 Apply sunscreen even in smoke-obscured conditions. UV radiation can still penetrate pollution and cause oxidative stress.

🔹Wash your face thoroughly and promptly after being outdoors to remove any pollutants.

🔹Shield your skin by wearing a face mask outside. Consider adding skincare improvements to your routine for added protection!

If you need to stock up on a good sunscreen or other face washes, creams, and serums - stop by our clinic! Our team would be happy to help you find the right products to help your skin fight off any harsh pollutants.

📍 2239 NE Doctors Dr Ste 100

How much sunscreen should you be using?☀️

It's more than you think! On just your face and neck, you should use half a teaspoon, which is equal to the amount most people use on their entire body.

Don't forget to reapply every 90 minutes for optimal protection! 😎

Vitamin C is a powerful antioxidant that applies its potent punch to not only help the skin fight off oxidative damage that causes some types of skin cancer but also helps to delay the damage that causes aging.

So how do you know which vitamin C serum is right for you? 🤔 The serum you use may depend on what works best for your skin; however, we recommend looking at the quality and concentration of a serum. It's best to stick to products that have concentrations ranging from 10% to 20% and have been backed with science to ensure the best results.

We carry two products of this amazing natural antioxidant here in the office!
🍊 C E ferulic by @skinceuticals
🍊 Alto by @skinbetter

Ask about these products during your next appointment or swing by our office to buy yours today!

A common question we get asked is: "What's the best sunscreen to use?"

Our answer is simple. The best sunscreen for you is the one that you like the best. Whether that be a powder, a lotion, a gel or even a spray.

At Juniper Dermatology, we love @eltamdskincare and use it on ourselves and our families. It comes in all different formulations. Come see the team and we will find the sunscreen that works best for you and your active Central Oregon lifestyle!

Andrea Westfall, BSN, is our Certified Nurse Injector! Her acute care nursing experience of ten years has given her a great desire to help people, treating patients with injectables and cosmetic fillers. Andrea’s goal while at your appointment is to make you feel comfortable and help with any of your cosmetic aging concerns, helping you achieve your goals. Andrea is also committed to education, whether learning about new products, injection techniques and medical grade skin care. She feels strongly in the power of injectables, believing they should also be seen as a preventative procedure to help slow the development of deep wrinkles!

We are very happy to announce Dacia Kailin, MSPAS, PA-C has joined our practice as a board-certified physician assistant.

She is an Oregon native and actually worked alongside Dr. Pivik as her medical assistant in Bend prior to PA school. Dacia enjoys practicing medicine with a person-centered approach and takes pride in her ability to treat every patient with empathy. Her schedule will be opening up soon and we are thrilled to have her on our team.

Full-body skin screening involves a simple office visit that usually takes between 15 and 20 minutes.

Your appointment will begin with us collecting information about your personal and family skin cancer and skin disease history. We will also need to know about any concerns or changes to your skin. Then we will visually inspect your skin, looking for any suspicious lesions that could indicate a cancerous or precancerous condition. During the exam, we might use a dermatoscope, which magnifies and applies special light to areas of the skin to aid in differentiating malignant and benign lesions!

Schedule your appointment today 📞 541-585-7546

Meet Tara McKenna, Certified Advanced Aesthetician! Tara has over 15 years of experience and is extremely knowledgeable in aesthetic skin care.

Prior to joining Juniper Dermatology, she worked as a Certified Advanced Aesthetician and Surgical Technician for a Dermatology and Plastic Surgery practice for a combined 15 years.

Tara is experienced in performing different types of non-invasive medical treatments including:
✨ IPL treatments
✨ ReFirme
✨ Microdermabrasion
✨ Microneedling
✨ Dermaplaning
✨ Chemical peels

In osteopathic medical schools, the philosophy is holistic, focusing on the whole person.

Osteopathic medicine emphasizes disease prevention through diet, exercise, and a healthy lifestyle. DOs receive extra training in osteopathic manipulative medicine (OMM), a physical skill used to diagnose and treat problems with the muscles, bones, and nerves. DOs focus on disease prevention and healthy living through nutrition and exercise.

October is breast cancer awareness month and while we focus on cancers of the skin, it’s important to remember your other screening exams for cancer. Full skin exams, mammograms, and colonoscopies are just a few ways you can stay on top of your health.

Make sure you are diligent and up to date with your breast cancer, skin cancer, and all other screenings. Your primary care provider can order your breast cancer screenings and you can visit Juniper Dermatology for your skin cancer exams!

Get to know Dr. Karla Pivik!

Dr. Karla Snider Pivik, FAAD, FAOCD, is a board-certified dermatologist who offers a patient-centered approach to treating dermatologic medical conditions.

Dr. Pivik follows the osteopathic philosophy that the body is a unit and all our systems are interrelated and contribute to our health. She values her patients as individuals and believes in providing exceptional care. She also understands the psychosocial impact dermatologic conditions can have and seeks to treat the whole person and the skin.

Our website has been updated and we are now scheduling patients via phone. We are so excited to see our returning patients and meet our new ones!
